const InteractiveDemo = () => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('hr');
  const [workflow, setWorkflow] = useState('draft'); 
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [showEncrypted, setShowEncrypted] = useState(false);
  const [candidateEmail, setCandidateEmail] = useState('');
  const [contractType, setContractType] = useState('HPP');
  const [employeeRole, setEmployeeRole] = useState('regular');
  const [probationMonths, setProbationMonths] = useState(3);
  const [durationMonths, setDurationMonths] = useState(12);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState('');
  const [workplace, setWorkplace] = useState('');
  const [weeklyHours, setWeeklyHours] = useState(40);
  const [salary, setSalary] = useState('');
  const [lawyerErrors, setLawyerErrors] = useState([]);
  const [isAutoFilling, setIsAutoFilling] = useState(false);
  const [pipelineInput, setPipelineInput] = useState("");
  const [pipelineStep, setPipelineStep] = useState(0); 
  const [payloadHex, setPayloadHex] = useState("");
  const [shamirLogs, setShamirLogs] = useState(["> Systém je připraven k inicializaci enterprise master klíče."]);
  const [vaultUnlocked, setVaultUnlocked] = useState(false);
  const [insertedShards, setInsertedShards] = useState([]);
  const [auditStep, setAuditStep] = useState(0); 

  useEffect(() => {
    const errors = [];
    if (workflow === 'draft' && candidateEmail && !candidateEmail.includes('@')) errors.push("Zadejte validní korporátní e-mail uchazeče.");
    if (contractType === 'HPP') {
      const maxProbation = employeeRole === 'manager' ? 6 : 3;
      if (probationMonths > maxProbation) errors.push(`Zkušební doba pro tuto pozici nesmí překročit ${maxProbation} měsíce (§ 35 ZP).`);
    }
    setLawyerErrors(errors);
  }, [candidateEmail, contractType, employeeRole, probationMonths, durationMonths]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPipelineInput(`INLOOPID_VC_METADATA:\nIssuer: Firma a.s.\nRole: ${employeeRole}\nPosition: ${position || 'N/A'}\nSalary_Tier: ${salary || 'N/A'} CZK\nWorkplace: ${workplace || 'N/A'}\nTimestamp: ${new Date().toISOString()}`);
  }, [employeeRole, position, salary, workplace]);

  const handleSmartAutoFill = () => {
    setIsAutoFilling(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setCandidateEmail('karel.novotny@enterprise.cz');
      setPosition('Senior Cloud Architekt');
      setWorkplace('Brno (HQ) / Hybrid');
      setSalary('120 000');
      setEmployeeRole('manager');
      setProbationMonths(6);
      setIsAutoFilling(false);
    }, 1500);
  };

  const executeSendToCandidate = () => {
    if(!candidateEmail || !position) return;
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => { setWorkflow('sent'); setIsProcessing(false); setActiveTab('candidate'); }, 1200);
  };

  const executeLoginMojeID = () => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => { setWorkflow('mojeid'); setIsProcessing(false); }, 1200);
  };

  const executeSignCandidate = () => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => { setWorkflow('candidate_signed'); setIsProcessing(false); setActiveTab('hr'); }, 1200);
  };

  const executeFinalApproveHR = () => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => { setWorkflow('completed'); setIsProcessing(false); setActiveTab('view'); }, 1500);
  };

  const executeCryptoPipeline = async () => {
    setPipelineStep(1);
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const data = encoder.encode(pipelineInput + Date.now().toString());
    const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-512', data);
    const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
    setTimeout(() => {
      setPayloadHex(hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('').toUpperCase());
      setPipelineStep(2);
      setTimeout(() => setPipelineStep(3), 1500);
    }, 1200);
  };

  const executeKeyCeremony = () => {
    setInsertedShards([]); setVaultUnlocked(false);
    setShamirLogs(["> Key Generation Ceremony dokončena.", "> 3 nezávislé šardy vygenerovány.", "> Pro přístup vyžadována přítomnost libovolných 2 autorit."]);
  };

  const injectShard = (id, label) => {
    if (insertedShards.includes(id)) return;
    const nextShards = [...insertedShards, id];
    setInsertedShards(nextShards);
    const currentLogs = [...shamirLogs, `> Importován ŠARD ${id}/3 [Autorita: ${label}] - Kontrolní součet validní.`];
    if (nextShards.length === 2) {
      currentLogs.push("> Dosažen kryptografický kvorum (K=2). Spouštím Lagrangeovu interpolaci nad GF(2^8)...");
      setShamirLogs(currentLogs);
      setTimeout(() => { setVaultUnlocked(true); setShamirLogs([...currentLogs, "> MASTER KEY REKONSTRUOVÁN. Izolovaná DB dešifrována a zpřístupněna."]); }, 1500);
    } else { setShamirLogs(currentLogs); }
  };

  const executeAuditEngine = () => {
    setAuditStep(1);
    setTimeout(() => { setAuditStep(2); setTimeout(() => { setAuditStep(3); setTimeout(() => setAuditStep(4), 1500); }, 1200); }, 1200);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col w-full min-h-[650px] bg-[#0B1120] text-slate-100 rounded-b-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-blue-950/50 shadow-2xl relative z-20">
      <div className="flex justify-start items-center bg-[#0A101D] border-b border-blue-900/30 px-4 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide select-none">
        {[
          { id: 'hr', label: '1. HR Terminál', icon: <Briefcase size={16}/> },
          { id: 'candidate', label: '2. Zaměstnanec', icon: <User size={16}/> },
          { id: 'zeroknowledge', label: '3. ZK Pipeline', icon: <Lock size={16}/> },
          { id: 'capsule', label: '4. T-Kapsle', icon: <Archive size={16}/> },
          { id: 'view', label: '5. Slepý notář', icon: <Eye size={16}/> },
          { id: 'audit', label: '6. eIDAS Audit', icon: <Scale size={16} className="text-emerald-400"/> }
        ].map((tab) => (
          <button key={tab.id} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)} className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-4 px-4 text-xs md:text-sm font-bold border-b-2 whitespace-nowrap transition-all ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'border-blue-500 text-blue-400 bg-[#111A3A]/50' : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300'}`}>{tab.icon} {tab.label}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 p-6 md:p-8">
        {activeTab === 'hr' && (
          <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
            <div className="border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-6 relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-6 gap-4">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2 text-white"><Briefcase size={20} className="text-blue-500"/> Korporátní editor kontraktů</h3>
                {workflow === 'draft' && ( <button onClick={handleSmartAutoFill} disabled={isAutoFilling} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-blue-300 border border-blue-700/50 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all disabled:opacity-50">{isAutoFilling ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin"/> : <Wand2 size={14}/>} Smart Vytěžení z CV</button> )}
              </div>
              {workflow === 'draft' && (
                <div className="space-y-6">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                    <div className="space-y-4">
                      <div><label className="block text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Komunikační e-mail</label><input type="email" value={candidateEmail} onChange={(e)=>setCandidateEmail(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-900/50 rounded-xl p-3 text-white text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="zadejte e-mail..." /></div>
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        <div><label className="block text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Pozice</label><input type="text" value={position} onChange={(e)=>setPosition(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-900/50 rounded-xl p-3 text-white text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="název..." /></div>
                        <div><label className="block text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Mzda (CZK)</label><input type="text" value={salary} onChange={(e)=>setSalary(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-900/50 rounded-xl p-3 text-white text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="hrubá mzda..." /></div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        <div><label className="block text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Role</label><select value={employeeRole} onChange={(e)=>setEmployeeRole(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-900/50 rounded-xl p-3 text-white text-sm outline-none"><option value="regular">Běžná</option><option value="manager">Management</option></select></div>
                        <div><label className="block text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Zkušebka (Měs.)</label><input type="number" value={probationMonths} onChange={(e)=>setProbationMonths(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-900/50 rounded-xl p-3 text-white text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"/></div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                      <div className="bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-900/30 rounded-xl p-5 flex-1 font-mono text-xs text-slate-400 space-y-3 relative">
                        <div className="text-white font-bold border-b border-blue-900/50 pb-2 uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px] flex items-center gap-2"><Zap size={12} className="text-amber-400"/> Automatická e-Identifikace</div>
                        <p className="flex justify-between items-center"><span className="text-slate-500">Jméno:</span> <span className="text-blue-400 italic">Doplní MojeID</span></p>
                        <p className="flex justify-between items-center"><span className="text-slate-500">Narození:</span> <span className="text-blue-400 italic">Doplní MojeID</span></p>
                        <p className="flex justify-between items-center"><span className="text-slate-500">Bydliště:</span> <span className="text-blue-400 italic">Doplní MojeID</span></p>
                      </div>
                      <div className={`p-4 rounded-xl border text-xs transition-all duration-300 ${lawyerErrors.length > 0 ? 'bg-rose-950/20 border-rose-800 text-rose-300' : 'bg-emerald-950/20 border-emerald-800/50 text-emerald-400'}`}>
                        <div className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">{lawyerErrors.length > 0 ? <AlertTriangle size={14}/> : <CheckCircle size={14}/>} Digitální Právník</div>
                        {lawyerErrors.length > 0 ? <ul className="list-disc pl-4">{lawyerErrors.map((err, i) => <li key={i}>{err}</li>)}</ul> : <p>V souladu se ZP.</p>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={executeSendToCandidate} disabled={isProcessing || lawyerErrors.length > 0 || !candidateEmail} className="w-full bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-600 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(29,78,216,0.3)] text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2">{isProcessing ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={16}/> : <Send size={16} />} Klientsky zašifrovat a odeslat návrh</button>
                </div>
              )}
              {(workflow === 'sent' || workflow === 'mojeid') && ( <div className="text-center py-16 space-y-4"><Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-blue-500" size={40} /><h4 className="font-bold text-white text-lg">Čekání na uchazeče</h4><p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Přepněte se do záložky "2. Zaměstnanec".</p></div> )}
              {workflow === 'candidate_signed' && ( <div className="space-y-6 text-center py-10"><CheckCircle className="mx-auto text-emerald-500" size={48}/><div><h4 className="font-bold text-white text-xl">Identita potvrzena</h4><p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mt-2">Zaměstnanec se bezpečně autorizoval přes NIA. Dokument je připraven k firemní pečeti.</p></div><button onClick={executeFinalApproveHR} disabled={isProcessing} className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-40 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><FileSignature size={16}/> Uzavřít a ukotvit v eIDAS</button></div> )}
              {workflow === 'completed' && ( <div className="text-center py-16"><Check size={48} className="mx-auto text-emerald-400 mb-4"/><h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Smlouva zalogována</h3><button onClick={()=>{setWorkflow('draft'); setActiveTab('hr');}} className="text-sm text-blue-400 hover:underline mt-4">Nový návrh</button></div> )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'candidate' && (
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-10 text-center animate-in fade-in">
            {workflow === 'draft' ? <div className="text-slate-500 py-10">Nejprve odešlete smlouvu z HR terminálu.</div> :
             workflow === 'sent' ? <div><h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-6">Máte nový návrh smlouvy</h3><button onClick={executeLoginMojeID} disabled={isProcessing} className="w-full bg-[#005AA8] hover:bg-[#004A8B] text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl flex justify-center items-center gap-2"><Fingerprint size={20}/> Ověřit totožnost (MojeID)</button></div> :
             workflow === 'mojeid' ? <div><h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-400 mb-6">Identita ověřena</h3><div className="bg-[#0B1120] text-left p-6 rounded-xl border border-blue-900/30 mb-6 font-mono text-sm space-y-2"><p><span className="text-slate-500">Jméno:</span> <span className="text-white">Karel Novotný</span></p><p><span className="text-slate-500">RČ:</span> <span className="text-white">900101/1234</span></p></div><button onClick={executeSignCandidate} disabled={isProcessing} className="w-full bg-blue-600 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold flex justify-center items-center gap-2"><PenTool size={20}/> Podepsat a odeslat</button></div> :
             <div className="text-blue-400 py-10 font-bold text-xl">Podpis úspěšně dokončen.</div>}
          </div>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'zeroknowledge' && (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-8 animate-in fade-in">
             <div className="border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-6"><h3 className="text-white font-bold mb-4 flex gap-2"><Laptop className="text-blue-500"/> Klientské zařízení</h3><div className="bg-[#0B1120] p-4 rounded-xl font-mono text-xs text-slate-300 h-40 whitespace-pre-wrap">{pipelineInput}</div><button onClick={executeCryptoPipeline} disabled={pipelineStep>0} className="w-full bg-blue-700 mt-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm">Zašifrovat lokálně</button></div>
             <div className="border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-6"><h3 className="text-white font-bold mb-4 flex gap-2"><Server className="text-slate-500"/> Servery InLoopID</h3><div className="bg-[#0B1120] p-4 rounded-xl font-mono text-xs text-emerald-500 h-40 break-all">{pipelineStep===0 ? "// Čekám..." : `U2FsdGVkX1+${payloadHex}A7B9F`}</div></div>
          </div>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'capsule' && (
          <div className="border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-10 text-center animate-in fade-in">
            <Archive size={48} className="mx-auto text-blue-500 mb-4"/>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-4">Shamir's Secret Sharing</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto mb-8">Při generování archivu je hlavní dešifrovací klíč rozdělen metodou 2-ze-3 mezi vedení firmy. Nezávislost na cloudu je zaručena.</p>
            <div className="flex justify-center gap-4">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-800 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400 font-bold">CEO</div>
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-[#0B1120] border border-emerald-800 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400 font-bold">HR</div>
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-[#0B1120] border border-amber-800 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 font-bold">LAW</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'view' && (
           <div className="border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto animate-in fade-in">
             <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6"><h3 className="text-white font-bold text-xl">Slepý notář v praxi</h3><button onClick={()=>setShowEncrypted(!showEncrypted)} className="bg-[#0B1120] border border-blue-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-blue-300">{showEncrypted ? "Dešifrovat zobrazení" : "Zobrazit DB stav"}</button></div>
             {showEncrypted ? <div className="bg-[#0B1120] p-6 rounded-xl font-mono text-emerald-500 text-xs break-all">U2FsdGVkX1+V0bW84YTM4OWI5NTRlM2M... [AES-256-GCM BLOB]</div> : <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-xl text-[#0B1120] font-serif shadow-xl"><h4 className="text-center font-black mb-4">PRACOVNÍ SMLOUVA</h4><p className="text-sm">Mezi společností Firma a.s. a Karlem Novotným...</p></div>}
           </div>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'audit' && (
           <div className="border border-blue-900/30 rounded-3xl bg-[#111A3A]/30 p-8 max-w-2xl mx-auto text-center animate-in fade-in">
              <Scale size={48} className="mx-auto text-emerald-500 mb-6"/>
              <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-6">Právní verifikace</h3>
              <button onClick={executeAuditEngine} disabled={auditStep>0} className="w-full bg-emerald-700 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-white mb-6">Ověřit integritu vůči Blockchainu</button>
              <div className="space-y-3 text-left max-w-md mx-auto font-mono text-xs">
                 <p className={auditStep>=1 ? "text-slate-300" : "text-slate-700"}>1. Získání lokálního hashe... {auditStep>1 && "OK"}</p>
                 <p className={auditStep>=2 ? "text-slate-300" : "text-slate-700"}>2. Validace eIDAS TSA... {auditStep>2 && "OK"}</p>
                 <p className={auditStep>=3 ? "text-amber-400" : "text-slate-700"}>3. Bitcoin OpenTimestamps Anchor... {auditStep>3 && "VERIFIED"}</p>
              </div>
           </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

 SOUBOR: ToastManager.jsx
 CESTA:  /data/data/com.termux/files/home/InloopID/frontend/src/components/ToastManager.jsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle, Info, X } from 'lucide-react';

export const ToastManager = () => {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleToast = (e) => {
      const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
      setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, ...e.detail }]);
      
      // Automatické smazání po 5 sekundách
      setTimeout(() => {
        setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id));
      }, 5000);
    };
    
    window.addEventListener('show-toast', handleToast);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('show-toast', handleToast);
  }, []);

  const removeToast = (id) => {
    setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-4 md:bottom-auto md:top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[110] flex flex-col gap-3 pointer-events-none w-full max-w-sm px-4">
      {toasts.map(t => (
        <div key={t.id} className={`relative flex items-start gap-3 px-5 py-4 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl pointer-events-auto transition-all duration-300 border overflow-hidden animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5 md:slide-in-from-top-5 fade-in zoom-in-95 backdrop-blur-xl
            ${t.type === 'error' ? 'bg-[#11050A]/90 border-rose-900/50 text-rose-200 shadow-[0_10px_40px_-10px_rgba(225,29,72,0.3)]' :
            t.type === 'success' ? 'bg-[#05110A]/90 border-emerald-900/50 text-emerald-200 shadow-[0_10px_40px_-10px_rgba(16,185,129,0.3)]' :
            'bg-[#0A101D]/90 border-blue-900/50 text-blue-200 shadow-[0_10px_40px_-10px_rgba(59,130,246,0.3)]'}`}>
          
          {t.type === 'error' ? <AlertTriangle size={20} className="text-rose-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" /> :
           t.type === 'success' ? <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-emerald-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" /> :
           <Info size={20} className="text-blue-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />}
          
          <div className="flex-1 text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed pr-6">{t.msg}</div>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => removeToast(t.id)} 
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-500 hover:text-white transition-colors outline-none"
            aria-label="Zavřít upozornění"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>

          {/* Progress bar indikátor */}
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-1 bg-white/5 w-full">
             <div className={`h-full ${t.type === 'error' ? 'bg-rose-500' : t.type === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-blue-500'}`} style={{ animation: 'toast-progress 5s linear forwards' }}></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
      <style dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{__html: `
        @keyframes toast-progress {
          from { width: 100%; }
          to { width: 0%; }
        }
      `}} />
    </div>
  );
};

export const notify = {
  error: (msg) => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-toast', {detail: {msg, type: 'error'}})),
  success: (msg) => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-toast', {detail: {msg, type: 'success'}})),
  info: (msg) => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-toast', {detail: {msg, type: 'info'}})),
};




================================================================================
 SOUBOR: ToastManager.jsx.bak_toast
 CESTA:  /data/data/com.termux/files/home/InloopID/frontend/src/components/ToastManager.jsx.bak_toast

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle, Info } from 'lucide-react';

export const ToastManager = () => {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleToast = (e) => {
      const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
      setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, ...e.detail }]);
      setTimeout(() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id)), 5000); // Zmizí po 5 sekundách
    };
    window.addEventListener('show-toast', handleToast);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('show-toast', handleToast);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[100] flex flex-col gap-2 pointer-events-none w-full max-w-md px-4">
      {toasts.map(t => (
        <div key={t.id} className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-4 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl pointer-events-auto transition-all duration-300 border ${
            t.type === 'error' ? 'bg-rose-950 border-rose-900 text-rose-200' :
            t.type === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-950 border-emerald-900 text-emerald-200' :
            'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-200'
        }`}>
          {t.type === 'error' ? <AlertTriangle size={24} className="text-rose-500 shrink-0"/> :
           t.type === 'success' ? <CheckCircle size={24} className="text-emerald-500 shrink-0"/> :
           <Info size={24} className="text-blue-500 shrink-0"/>}
          <span className="text-sm font-bold leading-tight">{t.msg}</span>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

export const notify = {
  error: (msg) => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-toast', {detail: {msg, type: 'error'}})),
  success: (msg) => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-toast', {detail: {msg, type: 'success'}})),
  info: (msg) => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-toast', {detail: {msg, type: 'info'}})),
};
